1	<!-- Logo placeholder: Raven logo will go here -->
2	<p align="center">
3	  <img src="docs/assets/logo-placeholder.png" alt="Raven" width="200" />
4	</p>
5	
6	<h1 align="center">Raven</h1>
7	<p align="center">Open-source multi-tenant knowledge base platform with AI-powered chat, voice, and WhatsApp</p>
8	
9	<p align="center">
10	  <a href="https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/actions/workflows/go.yml"><img src="https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/actions/workflows/go.yml/badge.svg" alt="Go CI" /></a>
11	  <a href="https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/actions/workflows/frontend.yml"><img src="https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/actions/workflows/frontend.yml/badge.svg" alt="Frontend CI" /></a>
12	  <a href="https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/actions/workflows/python.yml"><img src="https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/actions/workflows/python.yml/badge.svg" alt="Python CI" /></a>
13	  <a href="https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/actions/workflows/docker.yml"><img src="https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/actions/workflows/docker.yml/badge.svg" alt="Docker Build" /></a>
14	  <a href="https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/actions/workflows/security.yml"><img src="https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/actions/workflows/security.yml/badge.svg" alt="Security" /></a>
15	  <a href="https://codecov.io/gh/ravencloak-org/Raven"><img src="https://codecov.io/gh/ravencloak-org/Raven/branch/main/graph/badge.svg" alt="Coverage" /></a>
16	  <img src="https://img.shields.io/badge/license-TBD-lightgrey" alt="License" />
17	  <img src="https://img.shields.io/badge/PRs-welcome-blue" alt="PRs Welcome" />
18	</p>
19	
20	---
21	
22	## What is Raven?
23	
24	Raven is a self-hostable, multi-tenant knowledge base platform that lets organizations ingest documents and web content, then query them through AI-powered channels -- an embeddable chatbot, a real-time voice agent, and WhatsApp. It combines hybrid retrieval (vector search + BM25), BYOK LLM support, and a modular architecture designed for both cloud and edge deployment.
25	
26	The platform is organized around a clear hierarchy: **Organizations** (tenant boundaries) contain **Workspaces** (operational sub-units), which contain **Knowledge Bases** (collections of documents and web sources). Each layer enforces data isolation through PostgreSQL Row-Level Security, Keycloak-based authentication, and API middleware.
27	
28	Raven is built for teams that need a production-grade RAG platform without vendor lock-in. Bring your own LLM keys (Anthropic, OpenAI, Cohere), deploy on a cloud VM or a Raspberry Pi, and own your data end to end.
29	
30	## Key Features
31	
32	- **Multi-tenant by design** -- organization-level data isolation with RLS, per-tenant billing, and role-based access control
33	- **Embeddable chatbot** -- drop-in `<raven-chat>` web component with SSE streaming and domain-scoped API keys
34	- **Voice agent** -- real-time voice interface via LiveKit Agents with STT/TTS pipeline
35	- **WebRTC and WhatsApp** -- browser-based and WhatsApp Business Calling API integration
36	- **BYOK LLM** -- bring your own API keys for Anthropic Claude, OpenAI, Cohere, or self-hosted models
37	- **Hybrid search** -- pgvector cosine similarity + BM25 full-text search with Reciprocal Rank Fusion
38	- **Self-hostable** -- single Docker Compose deployment with no external SaaS dependencies required
39	- **Edge-deployable** -- Go API compiles to a ~25 MB ARM64 binary; run the API on a Raspberry Pi with a remote AI worker
40	
41	## Architecture Overview
42	
43	Raven uses a **two-process architecture**: a Go API server handles HTTP routing, authentication, and orchestration, while a Python AI worker handles all ML/AI workloads (embedding, RAG queries, document parsing, web scraping). The two communicate over gRPC, with Valkey (Redis-compatible) as the async job queue.
44	
45	PostgreSQL serves as the single source of truth -- storing relational data, vector embeddings (pgvector), and full-text search indexes. A Vue.js SPA provides the admin dashboard, and Keycloak handles identity management.
46	
47	## Tech Stack
48	
49	| Layer | Technology | Purpose |
50	|-------|-----------|---------|
51	| **API Server** | Go + Gin | REST API, JWT validation, tenant routing, SSE streaming |
52	| **AI Worker** | Python + gRPC | RAG queries, embeddings, document parsing, web scraping |
53	| **Database** | PostgreSQL 18 + pgvector | Relational data, vector search, BM25 full-text |
54	| **Frontend** | Vue.js 3 + Tailwind CSS | Admin dashboard (SPA, mobile-first, PWA-capable) |
55	| **Chatbot Widget** | Web Component | Embeddable `<raven-chat>` element |
56	| **Auth** | Keycloak | OIDC/OAuth2, user management, multi-tenant realms |
57	| **Job Queue** | Valkey (Redis fork) | Async document processing, caching, rate limiting |
58	| **Object Storage** | SeaweedFS | S3-compatible file storage (Apache 2.0) |
59	| **Voice** | LiveKit Server + Agents | WebRTC SFU, STT/LLM/TTS voice pipeline |
60	| **Reverse Proxy** | Traefik | Auto-TLS, routing, security headers |
61	
62	## Quick Start
63	
64	```bash
65	git clone https://github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven.git
66	cd Raven
67	cp .env.example .env        # fill in required values (see comments inside)
68	docker compose up -d        # starts all services
69	```
70	
71	The admin dashboard is available at `http://localhost:3000` once all containers are healthy. See [docs/quickstart.md](docs/quickstart.md) for a full walkthrough including first-user setup and Keycloak configuration.
72	
73	For local development without Docker, see [DEVELOPMENT.md](DEVELOPMENT.md).
74	
75	## Testing
76	
77	```bash
78	# Unit tests
79	make test
80	
81	# Integration tests (requires Docker — spins up pgvector via testcontainers)
82	make test-integration
83	
84	# Benchmarks (BM25, hybrid search, cache, ingestion throughput)
85	make bench-integration
86	
87	# Frontend E2E (Playwright)
88	cd frontend && npm run test:e2e
89	```
90	
91	**Integration test coverage** (47 test cases + 7 benchmarks across 5 suites):
92	
93	| Suite | Tests | What it covers |
94	|-------|-------|----------------|
95	| Ingestion | 20 | Document lifecycle (8-state machine), chunk/embedding storage, source creation, duplicates, concurrent ingestion, large docs (500 chunks), token accuracy |
96	| Search | 14 | BM25 keyword/phrase/filter/clamping, vector nearest-neighbor, hybrid RRF fusion/fallbacks, Unicode (CJK/emoji/RTL), duplicate embeddings |
97	| Cache | 10 | Valkey SHA256 exact-match, Postgres response_cache (hit_count, TTL, HNSW index, invalidation, Stats) |
98	| Benchmarks | 7 | BM25 p95 <100ms, hybrid p95 <200ms, HNSW p95 <50ms, ingestion <2s, token consistency |
99	| RLS | 8 | Document/chunk/embedding/cache/source tenant isolation, cross-org KB access, admin bypass |
100	
101	All integration tests run against a real PostgreSQL (pgvector) instance via testcontainers-go. Build tag `integration` keeps them out of `go test ./...`.
102	
103	## Roadmap
104	
105	Development is organized into five phases. See the full [design specification](docs/superpowers/specs/2026-03-27-raven-platform-design-final.md) for details.
106	
107	| Phase | Scope | Target |
108	|-------|-------|--------|
109	| **Phase 1** | MVP -- embeddable chatbot, document ingestion, hybrid search, admin dashboard | 8-12 weeks |
110	| **Phase 2** | Voice agent (LiveKit), smart response caching, email notifications | +4-6 weeks |
111	| **Phase 3** | WebRTC / WhatsApp Business Calling API integration | +4-6 weeks |
112	| **Phase 4** | Knowledge graph (Neo4j, multi-hop queries) | Future |
113	| **Phase 5** | Cloud managed offering, multi-region, SOC 2 | Future |
114	
115	## Contributing
116	
117	Contributions are welcome. Please open an issue to discuss proposed changes before submitting a pull request.
118	
119	- Read [CONTRIBUTING.md](CONTRIBUTING.md) for branch naming, commit style, and PR workflow
120	- Read [DEVELOPMENT.md](DEVELOPMENT.md) to get your local environment running
121	- Browse [open issues](../../issues) for tasks and bug reports
122	- See the [architecture overview](docs/wiki/Architecture-Overview.md) and [data model](docs/wiki/Data-Model.md) for context
123	
124	## License
125	
126	License TBD -- to be determined before public release.
127	
```

language: go\n            build-mode: autobuild\n          - language: python\n            build-mode: none\n          - language: javascript-typescript\n            build-mode: none\n    steps:\n      - name: Initialize CodeQL\n        uses: github/codeql-action/init@865f5f5c36632f18690a3d569fa0a764f2da0c3e  # v3\n        with:\n          languages: ${{ matrix.language }}\n          build-mode: ${{ matrix.build-mode }}\n          queries: security-extended\n\n      - name: Autobuild\n        if: matrix.build-mode == 'autobuild'\n        uses: github/codeql-action/autobuild@865f5f5c36632f18690a3d569fa0a764f2da0c3e  # v3\n\n      - name: Perform CodeQL Analysis\n        uses: github/codeql-action/analyze@865f5f5c36632f18690a3d569fa0a764f2da0c3e  # v3\n        with:\n          category: \"/language:${{ matrix.language }}\"\n"

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/raven/.worktrees/osps-gitleaks/.gitleaks.toml",
  "content": "# Gitleaks configuration for Raven.\n#\n# Extends the default ruleset and adds a repository-specific allowlist for\n# paths that legitimately contain placeholder credentials or third-party\n# artifacts that are reviewed out-of-band.\n\n[extend]\nuseDefault = true\n\n[allowlist]\ndescription = \"Raven repo-wide allowlist\"\n\n# Paths where scanner noise is expected and has been reviewed.\npaths = [\n  # Example / CI env files contain only placeholders like `changeme` or empty strings.\n  '''(?i)(^|/)\\.env\\.example$''',\n  '''(?i)(^|/)\\.env\\.ci$''',\n\n  # Fixtures, golden files, and test data — intentional inputs.\n  '''(?i)(^|/)testdata/''',\n  '''(?i)(^|/)fixtures/''',\n\n  # Vendored / third-party trees.\n  '''(?i)(^|/)vendor/''',\n  '''(?i)(^|/)node_modules/''',\n\n  # Local backup / scratch directories.\n  '''(?i)(^|/)backup/''',\n\n  # Lockfiles hash content (go.sum, bun.lock) — no secrets expected, high false-positive rate.\n  '''(^|/)go\\.sum$''',\n  '''(^|/)bun\\.lock$''',\n\n  # Generated worktrees — git metadata only.\n  '''(^|/)\\.worktrees/''',\n\n  # Gitleaks config itself may contain example regexes that look secret-like.\n  '''(^|/)\\.gitleaks\\.toml$''',\n]\n\n# Regexes that repeatedly trip on project-specific, non-secret strings.\nregexes = [\n  # SuperTokens development key pattern seen in .env.example; real prod\n  # secrets don't ship in the repo.\n  '''supertokens-dev-key-replace-me''',\n]\n"
}

  "new_string": "## Commit Style\n\n- Use [Conventional Commits](https://www.conventionalcommits.org/) — `feat:`, `fix:`, `chore:`, etc.\n- Keep the subject line under 72 characters\n- No AI attribution trailers (`Co-Authored-By:` etc.)\n- **Every commit must carry a `Signed-off-by:` trailer (DCO)** — see below\n\n## Developer Certificate of Origin (DCO)\n\nThis project requires every commit to be signed off under the [Developer Certificate of Origin 1.1](https://developercertificate.org/). Signing off certifies that you wrote the patch (or otherwise have the right to submit it under the project's open-source license).\n\nAdd the trailer automatically with `-s`:\n\n```bash\ngit commit -s -m \"feat: your message here\"\n```\n\nThis appends:\n\n```\nSigned-off-by: Your Name <your.email@example.com>\n```\n\nRules:\n\n- The sign-off email must match the email on your commit\n- Every commit on a PR must be signed — the `DCO` check blocks merge otherwise\n- To back-fill sign-offs on an existing branch: `git rebase --signoff main` then force-push\n- If you're committing on behalf of an employer, make sure your employer allows you to contribute under Apache 2.0 (the OSS license)\n\nThe DCO is enforced by a required CI check. See `.github/workflows/dco.yml`."
}

1	# Contributing to Raven
2	
3	## Before You Start
4	
5	Open an issue first for anything beyond a trivial fix. This prevents duplicate effort and lets us align on approach before you invest time writing code.
6	
7	## Branch Naming
8	
9	| Prefix | When |
10	|--------|------|
11	| `feat/` | New feature |
12	| `fix/` | Bug fix |
13	| `refactor/` | Code restructure, no behaviour change |
14	| `ci/` | CI/CD changes |
15	| `chore/` | Tooling, deps, config |
16	| `deps/` | Dependency bumps |
17	
18	Example: `feat/semantic-cache`, `fix/voice-session-timeout`
19	
20	## Commit Style
21	
22	- Use [Conventional Commits](https://www.conventionalcommits.org/) — `feat:`, `fix:`, `chore:`, etc.
23	- Keep the subject line under 72 characters
24	- No AI attribution trailers (`Co-Authored-By:` etc.)
25	
26	## Pull Request Workflow
27

  "new_string": "## Commit Style\n\n- Use [Conventional Commits](https://www.conventionalcommits.org/) — `feat:`, `fix:`, `chore:`, etc.\n- Keep the subject line under 72 characters\n- No AI attribution trailers (`Co-Authored-By:` etc.)\n- **Every commit must carry a `Signed-off-by:` trailer (DCO)** — see below\n\n## Developer Certificate of Origin (DCO)\n\nThis project requires every commit to be signed off under the [Developer Certificate of Origin 1.1](https://developercertificate.org/). Signing off certifies that you wrote the patch (or otherwise have the right to submit it under the project's open-source license).\n\nAdd the trailer automatically with `-s`:\n\n```bash\ngit commit -s -m \"feat: your message here\"\n```\n\nThis appends:\n\n```\nSigned-off-by: Your Name <your.email@example.com>\n```\n\nRules:\n\n- The sign-off email must match the email on your commit\n- Every commit on a PR must be signed — the `DCO` check blocks merge otherwise\n- To back-fill sign-offs on an existing branch: `git rebase --signoff main` then force-push\n- If you're committing on behalf of an employer, make sure your employer allows you to contribute under Apache 2.0 (the OSS license)\n\nThe DCO is enforced by a required CI check. See `.github/workflows/dco.yml`."
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/raven/.worktrees/osps-arch-deps-readme/docs/architecture.md",
  "content": "# Raven — Architecture Overview\n\nThis document describes the Raven system's actors, components, and trust boundaries at a level sufficient for a security reviewer or new contributor to reason about data flow and attack surface. It satisfies **OSPS-SA-01.01** from the [OpenSSF Baseline](https://baseline.openssf.org/versions/2026-02-19).\n\nFor a deeper feature-level walkthrough see [`docs/wiki/Architecture-Overview.md`](wiki/Architecture-Overview.md). For the historical design document see [`docs/superpowers/specs/`](superpowers/specs/).\n\n> **Threat model & attack-surface analysis** are deferred to Level 3 compliance and will be tracked in `docs/compliance/` once added.\n\n## System Diagram\n\n```mermaid\nflowchart LR\n    subgraph External\n      U[End users / admins]\n      EMB[Embedded chat widget]\n      WA[WhatsApp Business API]\n    end\n\n    subgraph Edge-or-cloud\n      FE[Vue 3 SPA<br/>admin dashboard]\n      API[Go + Gin API<br/>cmd/ + internal/]\n      AUTH[SuperTokens<br/>auth service]\n      QUE[Valkey<br/>Asynq queue]\n      WORK[Python gRPC<br/>AI worker<br/>ai-worker/]\n      DB[(PostgreSQL 18<br/>+ pgvector + BM25)]\n      OBJ[(SeaweedFS<br/>S3-compatible)]\n      LK[LiveKit<br/>SFU + agents]\n      OO[OpenObserve<br/>app telemetry]\n      BES[Beszel<br/>host metrics]\n    end\n\n    U --> FE\n    EMB --> API\n    WA --> API\n    FE --> API\n    API --> AUTH\n    API --> DB\n    API --> QUE\n    API --> WORK\n    API --> OBJ\n    API --> LK\n    WORK --> DB\n    WORK --> OBJ\n    QUE --> WORK\n    API -.-> OO\n    WORK -.-> OO\n    API -.-> BES\n```\n\n## Actors\n\n| Actor | Interaction | Trust level |\n|---|---|---|\n| End user (org member) | Uses the SPA or embedded widget | Low — authenticated via SuperTokens |\n| Admin (org owner) | Manages workspaces, KBs, API keys | Medium — session-scoped, MFA recommended |\n| API client (external system) | Calls `/api/v1/*` with API key | Low — key-scoped to a KB |\n| LLM provider (Anthropic / OpenAI / Cohere) | Called by AI worker with tenant-supplied keys (BYOK) | External — no credentials flow back to Raven |\n| AI worker | Embeds, retrieves, generates responses | Internal — trusted, sandboxed via gRPC boundary |\n| Ingestion job | Parses uploaded documents / scraped pages | Internal — untrusted input |\n| Observability agent (OpenObserve, Beszel) | Receives telemetry | Internal — one-way push |\n\n## Components\n\n| Component | Language / Runtime | Responsibility |\n|---|---|---|\n| API server | Go 1.26 + Gin | REST, auth, tenant routing, SSE streaming, RLS-scoped DB access |\n| AI worker | Python + gRPC | Embedding generation, hybrid retrieval (pgvector + BM25 + RRF), LLM orchestration, document parsing |\n| Frontend | Vue 3 + TypeScript + Tailwind | Admin dashboard (SPA), embeddable `<raven-chat>` web component |\n| Auth | SuperTokens | Email/password + OAuth (Google), session management, MFA |\n| Database | PostgreSQL 18 | Primary store; pgvector for embeddings, ParadeDB/BM25 for lexical search, RLS for tenant isolation |\n| Job queue | Valkey + Asynq (Go) | Asynchronous document processing, rate limiting, SHA256 response cache |\n| Object storage | SeaweedFS | S3-compatible file store for uploads + media |\n| Realtime media | LiveKit (server + agents) | WebRTC SFU, voice pipeline (STT → LLM → TTS) |\n| App telemetry | OpenObserve | Logs, traces, metrics via OpenTelemetry |\n| Host metrics | Beszel | Agent + hub for Raspberry Pi / VM host vitals |\n\n## Data Hierarchy\n\n```\nOrganization  (tenant boundary — PostgreSQL RLS)\n  └── Workspace  (operational sub-unit within org)\n       └── Knowledge Base  (collection of documents + web sources)\n            ├── Source  (upload, URL, scrape)\n            │    └── Document  (parsed content)\n            │         └── Chunk  (retrieval unit)\n            │              └── Embedding  (pgvector + BM25 index)\n            └── APIKey  (KB-scoped, used by embeddable widget / external clients)\n```\n\nRLS policies on `documents`, `chunks`, `embeddings`, `cache`, and `sources` enforce that `org_id` on every row matches the session's `app.org_id` setting. Cross-org reads return zero rows; cross-org writes fail.\n\n## Trust Boundaries\n\n1. **External ↔ API** — every request traverses the API's auth middleware. Embeddable widget traffic authenticates by API key bound to a specific KB; browser sessions use SuperTokens cookies.\n2. **API ↔ Data plane (DB, Valkey, object store)** — the API holds the only credentials and always sets `app.org_id` before any query. Direct DB access from any other component is forbidden.\n3. **API ↔ AI worker (gRPC)** — intra-network gRPC; the worker does not trust arbitrary callers but in practice is reachable only from the API.\n4. **Edge ↔ Cloud (optional)** — when deployed split between a Raspberry Pi (API) and a cloud AI worker, the gRPC channel is TLS-terminated; the edge node holds only short-lived session material.\n5. **User content ↔ LLM providers** — tenant-supplied BYOK credentials live in Postgres (encrypted at rest). The AI worker uses them for outbound calls; response content is stored in the per-KB cache when hit.\n\n## Core Data Flows\n\n### Document ingestion\n\n```\nuser → FE upload form → API /sources → SeaweedFS (blob)\n                              │\n                              ▼\n                        Valkey queue (ingest job)\n                              │\n                              ▼\n                        AI worker: parse → chunk → embed → persist\n                              │\n                              ▼\n                        PostgreSQL (chunks + embeddings)\n```\n\n### Chat / retrieval\n\n```\nuser → FE or embedded widget → API /chat\n                                  │\n                                  ▼\n                              AI worker (gRPC):\n                                1. Hybrid search (pgvector + BM25 + RRF)\n                                2. Response cache lookup (SHA256 of query)\n                                3. If miss: LLM call with retrieved context\n                                4. Store response in cache\n                                  │\n                                  ▼\n                              API → SSE stream → client\n```\n\n### Voice session\n\n```\nuser → FE WebRTC client → LiveKit SFU\n                             │\n                             ▼\n                        LiveKit agent (Python):\n                             STT → LLM (via AI worker) → TTS\n                             │\n                             ▼\n                        LiveKit SFU → audio back to user\n```\n\n## Security-Relevant Notes\n\n- **Secrets at rest** — tenant API keys (for LLM providers) encrypted with per-deployment AES-GCM; encryption key held in env (`RAVEN_SECRET_KEY`) or KMS.\n- **Session transport** — all external traffic is TLS (Traefik terminates); WebSockets and SSE inherit the outer TLS.\n- **Tenant isolation** — enforced at the row level by RLS (database layer), not relying on application-layer filtering alone.\n- **Dependency trust** — see [`docs/dependency-policy.md`](dependency-policy.md).\n- **Vulnerability disclosure** — see [`SECURITY.md`](../SECURITY.md).\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- **Enterprise Edition** (files prefixed `ee-`) are licensed separately and not part of the L2-compliance scope.\n- **Billing integrations** (Razorpay / Hyperswitch / UPI) use tenant-supplied gateway keys and inherit the BYOK trust model.\n"
}
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/jobinlawrance/Project/raven/.worktrees/osps-arch-deps-readme/docs/dependency-policy.md",
  "content": "# Dependency Policy\n\nHow Raven selects, obtains, and tracks its dependencies. Satisfies **OSPS-DO-06.01** from the [OpenSSF Baseline](https://baseline.openssf.org/versions/2026-02-19).\n\n## Selection Criteria\n\nNew dependencies are evaluated against:\n\n1. **License** — must be compatible with Apache 2.0 (the OSS project license). Preferred: Apache 2.0, MIT, BSD-2/3-Clause, ISC, MPL 2.0. Avoided without maintainer review: GPL / AGPL / SSPL / other copyleft.\n2. **Maintenance signal** — release within the last 12 months, active issue tracker, responsive to security reports.\n3. **Maintainer breadth** — prefer more than one active maintainer on the upstream.\n4. **Security track record** — no unresolved high-severity CVEs; a published security policy is a plus.\n5. **Footprint** — the minimum that solves the problem. Transitive dep count matters, especially for the edge/Raspberry Pi target.\n6. **Supply chain** — packages published under an identifiable org account; avoid long-tail single-author packages for security-sensitive paths.\n\n## Obtaining\n\n| Ecosystem | Source of truth | Lockfile | Registry |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| Go | `go.mod` | `go.sum` | proxy.golang.org (default) |\n| Python (ai-worker) | `ai-worker/pyproject.toml` (and/or `requirements*.txt`) | pip-tools output | PyPI |\n| Node / Bun (frontend) | `frontend/package.json` | `frontend/bun.lock` | npm registry |\n| Docker base images | `Dockerfile` and `ai-worker/Dockerfile` | tag pins | Docker Hub / GHCR official repos |\n| GitHub Actions | `.github/workflows/*.yml` | commit SHA pins | github.com |\n\nGitHub Actions are **pinned to commit SHA**, not floating tags. This prevents silent supply-chain attacks where a tag is retargeted.\n\n## Tracking\n\nAutomated monitoring covers each ecosystem weekly:\n\n- **[Dependabot](../.github/dependabot.yml)** — weekly PRs for `gomod`, `pip`, `npm`, `docker`, `github-actions`. Up to 10 open per ecosystem.\n- **[Trivy](../.github/workflows/security.yml)** — filesystem scan (Critical + High only) on every push/PR that touches Go/Python/Docker; weekly scheduled run on Mondays.\n- **[govulncheck](../.github/workflows/security.yml)** — Go-specific advisory check on every push/PR.\n- **[CodeQL](../.github/workflows/codeql.yml)** — SAST covering Go + Python + JS/TS on every PR and weekly.\n- **[gitleaks](../.github/workflows/gitleaks.yml)** — secret scanning on every push/PR and weekly.\n- **GitHub Advanced Security / Dependabot alerts** — in-repo advisories surfaced at `/security/dependabot`.\n\n## Review & Update Cadence\n\n- Dependabot PRs are reviewed within **7 days** of opening. Patch/minor bumps that pass CI auto-merge after review; major bumps require manual validation.\n- Any CVE with CVSS ≥ 7.0 is triaged within **72 hours** of alert receipt. If no fix is available upstream, document the mitigation in `.trivyignore` with rationale and an expiry date.\n- Unmaintained dependencies (no release in 18+ months + no response to a security issue) are candidates for replacement or vendoring.\n\n## Vendoring & Pinning\n\nRaven does not vendor Go modules. Lockfiles (`go.sum`, `bun.lock`, Python pinned requirements) are committed and reviewed.\n\nDocker base images pin by tag (e.g., `postgres:18.2-alpine`) rather than SHA for readability; tag movement is caught by Dependabot's docker ecosystem.\n\n## Quarantined / Exempted Dependencies\n\nEntries in [`.trivyignore`](../.trivyignore) document every known unfixed vulnerability plus the reason it is exempted (upstream has no patch, not reachable in our code path, etc.) and — where possible — a date at which we should revisit.\n\nA CVE entering `.trivyignore` requires:\n\n1. Confirming no upstream fix is available.\n2. A code-level check that the vulnerable path is not exercised in our usage, or a mitigation note.\n3. Maintainer review.\n\n## License Compliance at Release\n\nBefore any tagged release, `go-licenses` / `pip-licenses` / `license-checker` runs (or will run — see OpenSSF L2 Phase 4 work) produce a licenses manifest bundled with release artifacts. Apache-2.0-incompatible licenses in direct dependencies block the release.\n\n## Out of Scope\n\n- **Enterprise Edition** (`ee-*` files) may carry different dependency rules; covered separately.\n- **LLM provider APIs** — tenant-supplied at runtime (BYOK) and not part of the build-time dependency graph.\n\n## References\n\n- [`SECURITY.md`](../SECURITY.md) — vulnerability disclosure policy.\n- [`MAINTAINERS.md`](../MAINTAINERS.md) — who triages.\n- [OpenSSF Baseline 2026-02-19](https://baseline.openssf.org/versions/2026-02-19) — control taxonomy.\n"
}
```

===== ci-required.yml =====
name: CI Required

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  # Intentional Mergify marker job. Mergify gates merges on `#check-failure = 0`
  # across all workflows, so this job does not need `needs:` dependencies — it
  # is not the actual gate; it is a stable check name for branch-protection rules.
  gate:
    name: Gate
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - run: echo "CI gate passed"

===== claude.yml =====
name: Claude Code

on:
  # Manual: tag @claude in any comment/review to get help
  issue_comment:
    types: [created]
  pull_request_review_comment:
    types: [created]
  pull_request_review:
    types: [submitted]
  issues:
    types: [opened, assigned]

jobs:
  claude-assist:
    name: Claude (on-demand)
    if: |
      (github.event_name == 'issue_comment' && contains(github.event.comment.body, '@claude')) ||
      (github.event_name == 'pull_request_review_comment' && contains(github.event.comment.body, '@claude')) ||
      (github.event_name == 'pull_request_review' && contains(github.event.review.body, '@claude')) ||
      (github.event_name == 'issues' && (contains(github.event.issue.body, '@claude') || contains(github.event.issue.title, '@claude')))
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: write
      pull-requests: write
      issues: write
      id-token: write
      actions: read
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6
        with:
          fetch-depth: 0

      - name: Run Claude Code
        uses: anthropics/claude-code-action@v1
        with:
          claude_code_oauth_token: ${{ secrets.CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN }}
          additional_permissions: |
            actions: read

===== docker.yml =====
name: Docker Build & Push

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    tags: ['v*.*.*']
    paths:
      - 'Dockerfile'
      - 'ai-worker/Dockerfile'
      - 'frontend/Dockerfile'
      - 'frontend/nginx.conf'
      - 'docker-compose.yml'
      - '.github/workflows/docker.yml'
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - 'Dockerfile'
      - 'ai-worker/Dockerfile'
      - 'frontend/Dockerfile'
      - 'frontend/nginx.conf'
      - 'docker-compose.yml'
      - '.github/workflows/docker.yml'

permissions:
  contents: read
  packages: write

jobs:
  build-go-api:
    name: Build go-api
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - uses: docker/setup-qemu-action@v4

      - uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v4

      - name: Docker meta
        id: meta
        uses: docker/metadata-action@v6
        with:
          images: ghcr.io/${{ github.repository_owner }}/go-api
          tags: |
            type=ref,event=branch
            type=semver,pattern={{version}}
            type=semver,pattern={{major}}.{{minor}}
            type=sha,prefix=sha-
            type=raw,value=latest,enable={{is_default_branch}}

      - uses: docker/login-action@v4
        if: github.event_name != 'pull_request'
        with:
          registry: ghcr.io
          username: ${{ github.actor }}
          password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - uses: docker/build-push-action@v7
        with:
          context: .
          file: Dockerfile
          push: ${{ github.event_name != 'pull_request' }}
          platforms: linux/amd64,linux/arm64
          cache-from: type=gha
          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max
          tags: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}
          labels: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.labels }}

  build-python-worker:
    name: Build python-worker
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - uses: docker/setup-qemu-action@v4

      - uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v4

      - name: Docker meta
        id: meta
        uses: docker/metadata-action@v6
        with:
          images: ghcr.io/${{ github.repository_owner }}/python-worker
          tags: |
            type=ref,event=branch
            type=semver,pattern={{version}}
            type=semver,pattern={{major}}.{{minor}}
            type=sha,prefix=sha-
            type=raw,value=latest,enable={{is_default_branch}}

      - uses: docker/login-action@v4
        if: github.event_name != 'pull_request'
        with:
          registry: ghcr.io
          username: ${{ github.actor }}
          password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - uses: docker/build-push-action@v7
        with:
          context: ai-worker
          file: ai-worker/Dockerfile
          push: ${{ github.event_name != 'pull_request' }}
          platforms: linux/amd64,linux/arm64
          cache-from: type=gha
          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max
          tags: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}
          labels: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.labels }}

  build-frontend:
    name: Build frontend
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - uses: docker/setup-qemu-action@v4

      - uses: docker/setup-buildx-action@v4

      - name: Docker meta
        id: meta
        uses: docker/metadata-action@v6
        with:
          images: ghcr.io/${{ github.repository_owner }}/frontend
          tags: |
            type=ref,event=branch
            type=semver,pattern={{version}}
            type=semver,pattern={{major}}.{{minor}}
            type=sha,prefix=sha-
            type=raw,value=latest,enable={{is_default_branch}}

      - uses: docker/login-action@v4
        if: github.event_name != 'pull_request'
        with:
          registry: ghcr.io
          username: ${{ github.actor }}
          password: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

      - uses: docker/build-push-action@v7
        with:
          context: frontend
          file: frontend/Dockerfile
          push: ${{ github.event_name != 'pull_request' }}
          platforms: linux/amd64,linux/arm64
          cache-from: type=gha
          cache-to: type=gha,mode=max
          build-args: |
            VITE_API_URL=${{ vars.VITE_API_URL || 'https://api.ravencloak.org' }}
            VITE_API_BASE_URL=${{ vars.VITE_API_BASE_URL || 'https://api.ravencloak.org/api/v1' }}
            VITE_LIVEKIT_URL=${{ vars.VITE_LIVEKIT_URL || '' }}
          tags: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.tags }}
          labels: ${{ steps.meta.outputs.labels }}

===== frontend.yml =====
name: Frontend CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - 'frontend/**'
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - 'frontend/**'

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  build:
    name: Lint & Build
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: frontend
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - uses: actions/setup-node@v6
        with:
          node-version: '22'
          cache: npm
          cache-dependency-path: frontend/package-lock.json

      - run: npm ci

      - run: npm run lint

      - run: npx vue-tsc --noEmit

      - run: npm run build

  e2e:
    name: Playwright E2E
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    needs: build
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: frontend
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - uses: actions/setup-node@v6
        with:
          node-version: '22'
          cache: npm
          cache-dependency-path: frontend/package-lock.json

      - name: Install dependencies
        run: npm ci

      - name: Vitest unit tests (fast-fail gate)
        run: npm run test:unit

      - name: Install Playwright browsers
        run: npx playwright install --with-deps chromium

      - name: Build for preview
        run: npm run build

      # ------------------------------------------------------------------
      # Required secrets for Playwright E2E tests
      # ------------------------------------------------------------------
      # API_BASE_URL   — base URL of the running Raven API (e.g. https://api.example.com)
      # KEYCLOAK_URL   — base URL of the Keycloak instance for OIDC auth
      # E2E_USER       — username of the regular test user
      # E2E_PASS       — password of the regular test user
      # E2E_ADMIN      — username of the admin test user
      # E2E_ADMIN_PASS — password of the admin test user
      # E2E_API_KEY    — pre-generated API key for token-authenticated requests
      # E2E_KB_ID      — knowledge-base ID used in retrieval E2E flows
      # E2E_KB_A_KEY   — API key scoped to knowledge-base A
      # E2E_KB_B_ID    — knowledge-base B ID used in multi-KB E2E flows
      # META_WEBHOOK_SECRET — HMAC secret for verifying Meta webhook payloads
      #
      # When these secrets are missing (forks, new environments, or PRs
      # from external contributors), every API-dependent E2E test will
      # silently skip because the test fixtures guard on the presence of
      # these environment variables.  The Playwright run will still pass,
      # but with zero API coverage.
      # ------------------------------------------------------------------
      - name: Verify E2E secrets
        if: github.event_name != 'pull_request' || github.event.pull_request.head.repo.full_name == github.repository
        run: |
          missing=""
          [ -z "$E2E_USER" ]       && missing="$missing E2E_USER"
          [ -z "$E2E_PASS" ]       && missing="$missing E2E_PASS"
          [ -z "$E2E_ADMIN" ]      && missing="$missing E2E_ADMIN"
          [ -z "$E2E_ADMIN_PASS" ] && missing="$missing E2E_ADMIN_PASS"
          [ -z "$API_BASE_URL" ]   && missing="$missing API_BASE_URL"
          [ -z "$KEYCLOAK_URL" ]   && missing="$missing KEYCLOAK_URL"
          if [ -n "$missing" ]; then
            echo "::warning::E2E secrets missing:$missing — tests will be skipped"
          fi
        env:
          E2E_USER: ${{ secrets.E2E_USER }}
          E2E_PASS: ${{ secrets.E2E_PASS }}
          E2E_ADMIN: ${{ secrets.E2E_ADMIN }}
          E2E_ADMIN_PASS: ${{ secrets.E2E_ADMIN_PASS }}
          API_BASE_URL: ${{ secrets.API_BASE_URL }}
          KEYCLOAK_URL: ${{ secrets.KEYCLOAK_URL }}

      - name: Playwright E2E tests
        run: npm run test:e2e
        env:
          CI: true
          E2E_USER: ${{ secrets.E2E_USER }}
          E2E_PASS: ${{ secrets.E2E_PASS }}
          E2E_ADMIN: ${{ secrets.E2E_ADMIN }}
          E2E_ADMIN_PASS: ${{ secrets.E2E_ADMIN_PASS }}
          E2E_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.E2E_API_KEY }}
          E2E_KB_ID: ${{ secrets.E2E_KB_ID }}
          E2E_KB_A_KEY: ${{ secrets.E2E_KB_A_KEY }}
          E2E_KB_B_ID: ${{ secrets.E2E_KB_B_ID }}
          META_WEBHOOK_SECRET: ${{ secrets.META_WEBHOOK_SECRET }}
          API_BASE_URL: ${{ secrets.API_BASE_URL }}          # required for API E2E tests
          KEYCLOAK_URL: ${{ secrets.KEYCLOAK_URL }}          # required for API E2E tests

      - name: Upload Playwright report
        if: failure()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
        with:
          name: playwright-report
          path: frontend/playwright-report/

===== go.yml =====
name: Go CI

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - 'cmd/**'
      - 'internal/**'
      - 'pkg/**'
      - 'tests/**'
      - 'go.mod'
      - 'go.sum'
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - 'cmd/**'
      - 'internal/**'
      - 'pkg/**'
      - 'tests/**'
      - 'go.mod'
      - 'go.sum'

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  build-and-test:
    name: Build & Test
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - uses: actions/setup-go@v6
        with:
          go-version: '1.26.2'
          cache: true

      - run: go mod download

      - name: Install dotenvx
        run: curl -sfS https://dotenvx.sh | sh

      - name: Install eBPF build tools
        run: sudo apt-get install -y clang llvm libbpf-dev linux-tools-$(uname -r) || sudo apt-get install -y clang llvm libbpf-dev

      - name: Pre-pull testcontainers image
        run: docker pull pgvector/pgvector:pg18

      - run: CGO_ENABLED=1 go build ./cmd/api

      - name: Prepare coverage directories
        run: mkdir -p .coverdata/unit .coverdata/migrations .coverdata/integration coverage

      - name: Run tests
        run: |
          if [ -n "$DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI" ]; then
            dotenvx run -f .env.ci -- go test -race -timeout 30m \
              -cover -coverpkg="$COV_PKGS" ./... \
              -args -test.gocoverdir="$PWD/.coverdata/unit"
          else
            echo "::warning::DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI not set (fork PR?) — running tests without dotenvx"
            go test -race -timeout 30m \
              -cover -coverpkg="$COV_PKGS" ./... \
              -args -test.gocoverdir="$PWD/.coverdata/unit"
          fi
        env:
          COV_PKGS: github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/...,github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/pkg/...,github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/cmd/...
          DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI: ${{ secrets.DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI }}
          TESTCONTAINERS_RYUK_DISABLED: "true"

      - name: Run migration tests
        run: |
          if [ -n "$DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI" ]; then
            dotenvx run -f .env.ci -- go test -race -run '^TestMigrationsUpAndDown$' \
              -cover -coverpkg="$COV_PKGS" ./migrations/... \
              -args -test.gocoverdir="$PWD/.coverdata/migrations"
          else
            go test -race -run '^TestMigrationsUpAndDown$' \
              -cover -coverpkg="$COV_PKGS" ./migrations/... \
              -args -test.gocoverdir="$PWD/.coverdata/migrations"
          fi
        env:
          COV_PKGS: github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/...,github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/pkg/...,github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/cmd/...
          DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI: ${{ secrets.DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI }}
          TESTCONTAINERS_RYUK_DISABLED: "true"

      - name: Run integration tests with coverage
        run: |
          go test -tags=integration -race -timeout 5m \
            -cover -coverpkg="$COV_PKGS" \
            ./internal/integration/ \
            -args -test.gocoverdir="$PWD/.coverdata/integration"
        env:
          COV_PKGS: github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/...,github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/pkg/...,github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/cmd/...
          TESTCONTAINERS_RYUK_DISABLED: "true"

      - name: Merge coverage profiles
        if: always()
        run: |
          INPUTS=""
          for d in unit migrations integration; do
            if compgen -G ".coverdata/$d/cov*" >/dev/null; then
              INPUTS="${INPUTS:+$INPUTS,}.coverdata/$d"
            fi
          done
          if [ -z "$INPUTS" ]; then
            echo "::warning::no coverage data collected"
            exit 0
          fi
          echo "merging: $INPUTS"
          go tool covdata textfmt -i="$INPUTS" -o=coverage/cov.out
          go tool covdata percent -i="$INPUTS" | tee coverage/summary.txt
          go tool cover -func=coverage/cov.out | tail -1 | tee -a coverage/summary.txt
          go tool cover -html=coverage/cov.out -o coverage/coverage.html

      - name: Upload coverage artifacts
        if: always()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
        with:
          name: coverage-data
          path: |
            .coverdata/
            coverage/
          retention-days: 5

      - uses: codecov/codecov-action@v6
        if: always()
        with:
          token: ${{ secrets.CODECOV_TOKEN }}
          files: coverage/cov.out
          fail_ci_if_error: false

      - run: go vet ./...

      - name: Verify generated eBPF files are up-to-date
        run: |
          if [ -f /sys/kernel/btf/vmlinux ] && bpftool btf dump file /sys/kernel/btf/vmlinux format c > internal/ebpf/programs/vmlinux.h 2>/dev/null; then
            go generate ./internal/ebpf/...
            git diff --exit-code -- internal/ebpf ':(exclude)internal/ebpf/programs/vmlinux.h' || (echo "Generated eBPF artifacts are out of date. Run 'go generate ./internal/ebpf/...'" && exit 1)
          else
            echo "::warning::BTF or bpftool not available on this runner — skipping eBPF artifact verification"
          fi

  lint:
    name: Lint
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - uses: actions/setup-go@v6
        with:
          go-version: '1.26.2'
          cache: true

      - name: Install eBPF build tools
        run: sudo apt-get install -y clang llvm libbpf-dev linux-tools-$(uname -r) || sudo apt-get install -y clang llvm libbpf-dev

      - uses: golangci/golangci-lint-action@v9
        with:
          version: v2.11.4

  ebpf-integration:
    name: eBPF Integration Tests
    # ubuntu-latest runners have kernel ≥ 6.x with BTF and sufficient caps for eBPF
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - uses: actions/setup-go@v6
        with:
          go-version: '1.26.2'
          cache: true

      - name: Install eBPF build tools
        run: sudo apt-get install -y clang llvm libbpf-dev

      - name: Run eBPF integration tests
        run: go test -race -v ./internal/ebpf/... ./cmd/api/...

  ebpf-privileged-tests:
    name: eBPF Privileged Test Harness
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    continue-on-error: true
    # Only run on pushes to main — these tests require privileged containers
    if: github.event_name == 'push' && github.ref == 'refs/heads/main'
    permissions:
      contents: read
    container:
      image: ubuntu:24.04
      options: --privileged
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - name: Install system dependencies
        run: |
          apt-get update
          apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
            clang llvm libbpf-dev linux-tools-$(uname -r) linux-tools-generic \
            ca-certificates curl git docker.io

      - uses: actions/setup-go@v6
        with:
          go-version: '1.26.2'
          cache: true

      - run: go mod download

      - name: Run privileged eBPF tests
        run: |
          go test -tags ebpf -race -v -timeout 10m \
            ./tests/ebpf/xdp/... \
            ./tests/ebpf/observability/... \
            ./tests/ebpf/audit/...

===== landing.yml =====
name: Deploy Landing to Cloudflare Pages

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - "landing/**"
      - ".github/workflows/landing.yml"

concurrency:
  group: landing-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  deploy:
    name: Deploy
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
      deployments: write

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - name: Deploy to Cloudflare Pages
        env:
          CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
        if: env.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN != ''
        uses: cloudflare/wrangler-action@v3
        with:
          apiToken: ${{ env.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
          accountId: ${{ vars.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}
          command: pages deploy landing --project-name=raven-landing --branch=main --commit-dirty=true

===== opencode.yml =====
name: opencode

on:
  issue_comment:
    types: [created]
  pull_request_review_comment:
    types: [created]

jobs:
  opencode:
    if: |
      contains(github.event.comment.body, ' /oc') ||
      startsWith(github.event.comment.body, '/oc') ||
      contains(github.event.comment.body, ' /opencode') ||
      startsWith(github.event.comment.body, '/opencode')
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      id-token: write
      contents: read
      pull-requests: read
      issues: read
    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v6
        with:
          persist-credentials: false

      - name: Run opencode
        uses: anomalyco/opencode/github@latest
        env:
          ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY }}
        with:
          model: anthropic/claude-opus-4-5
===== pages.yml =====
name: Deploy Frontend to Cloudflare Pages

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - "frontend/**"
      - ".github/workflows/pages.yml"
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - "frontend/**"
      - ".github/workflows/pages.yml"

concurrency:
  group: pages-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  build-and-deploy:
    name: Build & Deploy
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    permissions:
      contents: read
      deployments: write
      pull-requests: write

    steps:
      - name: Checkout
        uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - name: Setup Node.js
        uses: actions/setup-node@v6
        with:
          node-version: "22"
          cache: "npm"
          cache-dependency-path: frontend/package-lock.json

      - name: Install dependencies
        working-directory: frontend
        run: npm ci

      - name: Lint
        working-directory: frontend
        run: npm run lint

      - name: Build
        working-directory: frontend
        run: npm run build
        env:
          VITE_API_URL: ${{ vars.VITE_API_URL || 'https://api.ravencloak.org' }}
          VITE_API_BASE_URL: ${{ vars.VITE_API_BASE_URL || 'https://api.ravencloak.org/api/v1' }}
          VITE_API_DOMAIN: ${{ vars.VITE_API_DOMAIN || 'https://api.ravencloak.org' }}
          VITE_LIVEKIT_URL: ${{ vars.VITE_LIVEKIT_URL || '' }}

      - name: Deploy to Cloudflare Pages
        # Only deploy on push to main when the API token is available.
        env:
          CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
        if: github.event_name == 'push' && github.ref == 'refs/heads/main' && env.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN != ''
        uses: cloudflare/wrangler-action@v3
        with:
          apiToken: ${{ env.CLOUDFLARE_API_TOKEN }}
          accountId: ${{ vars.CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID }}
          command: pages deploy frontend/dist --project-name=raven-frontend --commit-dirty=true
          gitHubToken: ${{ secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}

===== python.yml =====
name: Python CI

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - 'ai-worker/**'
      - 'proto/**'
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - 'ai-worker/**'
      - 'proto/**'
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  test:
    name: Lint, Type-check & Test
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: ai-worker
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - uses: actions/setup-python@v6
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'
          cache: pip
          cache-dependency-path: ai-worker/pyproject.toml

      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"

      - run: ruff check

      - run: ruff format --check

      - run: mypy raven_worker

      - name: Install dotenvx
        run: curl -sfS https://dotenvx.sh | sh

      - name: Run tests
        run: |
          if [ -n "$DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI" ]; then
            dotenvx run -f ${{ github.workspace }}/.env.ci -- pytest -v --cov=raven_worker --cov-report=xml --cov-report=term-missing
          else
            echo "::warning::DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI not set (fork PR?) — running tests without dotenvx"
            pytest -v --cov=raven_worker --cov-report=xml --cov-report=term-missing
          fi
        env:
          DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI: ${{ secrets.DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI }}

      - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v7
        with:
          name: coverage-report
          path: ai-worker/coverage.xml
          retention-days: 7

  smoke-tests:
    if: github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    environment: smoke
    defaults:
      run:
        working-directory: ai-worker
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - uses: actions/setup-python@v6
        with:
          python-version: '3.12'

      - run: pip install -e ".[dev]"

      - name: Install dotenvx
        run: curl -sfS https://dotenvx.sh | sh

      - name: Run LLM provider smoke tests
        env:
          DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI: ${{ secrets.DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI }}
        run: |
          if [ -n "$DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI" ]; then
            dotenvx run -f ${{ github.workspace }}/.env.ci -- bash -euo pipefail -c '
              : "${OPENAI_API_KEY:?OPENAI_API_KEY missing from .env.ci}"
              : "${COHERE_API_KEY:?COHERE_API_KEY missing from .env.ci}"
              : "${ANTHROPIC_API_KEY:?ANTHROPIC_API_KEY missing from .env.ci}"
              python -m pytest tests/smoke/ -v --tb=short
            '
          else
            echo "::warning::DOTENV_PRIVATE_KEY_CI not set — skipping smoke tests"
            exit 0
          fi

===== security.yml =====
name: Security Scanning

on:
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - '**.go'
      - 'go.mod'
      - 'go.sum'
      - 'ai-worker/**'
      - 'Dockerfile'
      - 'ai-worker/Dockerfile'
      - '.github/workflows/security.yml'
  pull_request:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - '**.go'
      - 'go.mod'
      - 'go.sum'
      - 'ai-worker/**'
      - 'Dockerfile'
      - 'ai-worker/Dockerfile'
      - '.github/workflows/security.yml'
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 6 * * 1'  # Weekly on Monday at 06:00 UTC

permissions:
  contents: read
  security-events: write

jobs:
  trivy-scan:
    name: Trivy Filesystem Scan
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - name: Run Trivy vulnerability scanner
        uses: aquasecurity/trivy-action@v0.35.0
        with:
          scan-type: fs
          scan-ref: .
          severity: CRITICAL,HIGH
          exit-code: 1
          format: table

  go-vuln:
    name: Go Vulnerability Check
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v6

      - name: Setup Go
        uses: actions/setup-go@v6
        with:
          go-version: '1.26.2'
          cache: true

      - name: Install govulncheck
        run: go install golang.org/x/vuln/cmd/govulncheck@v1.1.4

      - name: Run govulncheck
        # Exclude internal/testutil: it imports testcontainers (docker/docker transitive dep)
        # which has no upstream fix (GO-2026-4887). testutil is test-infrastructure only and
        # never compiled into production binaries.
        # continue-on-error: pgx/v5 CVEs have "Fixed in: N/A" upstream;
        # block will re-tighten once upstream patches land.
        continue-on-error: true
        run: |
          govulncheck -scan=package \
            $(go list ./... | grep -vE '/testutil$')

Implements:\n\n  * OSPS-AC-04.01 — CI/CD least privilege default\n  * OSPS-BR-01.01 — sanitize untrusted CI metadata (none found in\n    run: blocks; all github.event.* references live in if: guards)\n  * OSPS-BR-01.03 — prevent privileged credentials on untrusted\n    code (no pull_request_target usage found)\n\nPer-job grant matrix:\n\n  | Workflow              | Jobs                | Grants                                         |\n  |-----------------------|---------------------|-------------------------------------------------|\n  | ci-required.yml       | gate                | contents: read                                 |\n  | claude.yml            | claude-assist       | contents+issues+pull-requests: write, id-token: write, actions: read (as existing) |\n  | docker.yml            | build-go-api, build-python-worker, build-frontend | contents: read, packages: write |\n  | frontend.yml          | build, e2e          | contents: read                                 |\n  | go.yml                | build-and-test, lint, ebpf-integration, ebpf-privileged-tests | contents: read |\n  | landing.yml           | deploy              | contents: read, deployments: write (as existing) |\n  | opencode.yml          | opencode            | id-token: write, contents: read, pull-requests+issues: read (as existing) |\n  | pages.yml             | build-and-deploy    | contents: read, deployments: write, pull-requests: write (as existing) |\n  | python.yml            | test, smoke-tests   | contents: read                                 |\n  | security.yml          | trivy-scan, go-vuln | contents: read                                 |\n\nNo functional changes — only permissions tightening.\" && \\\ngit push -u origin ci/osps-workflow-permissions 2>&1 | tail -3 && \\\ngh pr create --title \"ci(osps): lock down workflow permissions to least privilege\" --body \"$(cat <<'EOF'\n## Summary\n\nAudits and locks down `permissions:` on all 10 existing workflows. Every workflow now has **top-level `permissions: {}`** plus explicit per-job grants at the minimum each job actually needs.\n\nImplements **OSPS-AC-04.01**, **OSPS-BR-01.01**, and **OSPS-BR-01.03** from the [OpenSSF Baseline L2](https://baseline.openssf.org/versions/2026-02-19) target (see #330).\n\n## Scans\n\n- **`pull_request_target` usage:** none found (BR-01.03 clean)\n- **Unsafe `${{ github.event.* }}` interpolation in `run:` blocks:** none found. All references are in `if:` guards, where interpolation is safe.\n\n## Per-job grant matrix\n\n| Workflow | Jobs | Grants |\n|---|---|---|\n| `ci-required.yml` | `gate` | `contents: read` |\n| `claude.yml` | `claude-assist` | unchanged: wide perms for code assistant |\n| `docker.yml` | 3 build jobs | `contents: read`, `packages: write` |\n| `frontend.yml` | `build`, `e2e` | `contents: read` |\n| `go.yml` | `build-and-test`, `lint`, 2 eBPF jobs | `contents: read` |\n| `landing.yml` | `deploy` | unchanged: `contents: read`, `deployments: write` |\n| `opencode.yml` | `opencode` | unchanged: `id-token: write`, read-only for others |\n| `pages.yml` | `build-and-deploy` | unchanged |\n| `python.yml` | `test`, `smoke-tests` | `contents: read` |\n| `security.yml` | `trivy-scan`, `go-vuln` | `contents: read` (removed job-wide `security-events: write` — no job currently uploads SARIF; re-add when we wire SARIF upload) |\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [ ] All workflows pass YAML parse (verified locally — 10/10 OK)\n- [ ] CI runs on this PR and all jobs complete without new permission errors\n- [ ] If any job fails with a permissions error, widen that specific job's grant and re-push\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -3",
  "description": "Commit + push + PR for permissions lockdown"
}
